\n|previousevent=\n|followingevent=\n}}\n'''''ONE Friday Fights 119: Samingdam vs. Sonrak''''' (also known as '''''ONE Lumpinee 119''''') was a [[combat sport]]s event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on August 8, 2025, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n===background===\nA flyweight Muay Thai bout between [[Gingsanglek Tor.Laksong]] and Thant Zin was scheduled to headline the event, but the bout was cancelled due Gingsanglek having an illness, and the bout was instead replaced by Samingdam N.F.Looksuan and Sonrak Fairtex.{{Cite web [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n===background===\nA bantamweight Muay Thai bout between Felipe Lobo and [[Kulabdam Sor.Jor.Piek-U-Thai]] was headlined the event.{{Cite web |date=2025-09-03 |title=Full Card The Home Of Martial Arts |language=en-US}} The bout was originally scheduled at [[#ONE Friday Fights 114|ONE Friday Fights 114]] on June 27, but was cancelled after Kulabdam had an injury.\n\n===Bonus (unanimous)\n|align=center|1\n|align=center|10:00\n|\n|}\n\n==ONE Friday Fights 137==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=ONE Friday Fights 137: Tawanchai vs. Liu\n|image=\n|image_size=320\n|promotion=[[ONE Championship]]\n|date={{start date|2025|12|19}}\n|venue=[[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]]\n|city=[[Bangkok]], Thailand \n|previousevent=\n|followingevent=\n}}\n'''''ONE Friday Fights 137: Tawanchai vs. Liu''''' (also known as '''''ONE Lumpinee 137''''') was a [[combat sport]]s event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on December 19, 2025, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n===background===\nA featherweight Kickboxing bout between current [[ONE Featherweight Muay Thai World Championship|ONE Featherweight Muay Thai World Champion]] [[Tawanchai P.K. Saenchaimuaythaigym|Tawanchai P.K.Saenchai]] and Liu Mengyang was headlined the event.{{Cite web |date=2025-12-14 |title=Full Card Revealed for ONE Friday Fights 137 on December 19\n|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/full-card-revealed-for-one-friday-fights-137-on-december-19/ |access-date=2025-12-15 |website=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home Of Martial Arts |language=en-US}}\n\n===Bonus awards===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses:\n*'''Performance of the Night: Sam-A Gaiyanghadao, Mohammad Siasarani, Yod-IQ Or.Pimonsri, Gingsanglek Tor.Laksong, Suriyanlek Por.Yenying''' and '''Duangdawnoi Looksaikongdin'''\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|- \n! colspan=\"8\" style=\"background-color: #ccf; color: #000080; text-align: center;\" | '''ONE Friday Fights 137 (YouTube / Watch ONE)'''\n|-\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Weight Class\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | \n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | \n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | \n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Method\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Round\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Time\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Notes\n|-\n|Featherweight Kickboxing\n|{{flagicon|CHN}} Liu Mengyang\n|def.\n|{{flagicon|THA}} [[Tawanchai P.K.
